  "prompt_tokens": 2525,
  "raw_model_response": "To determine how much my manager paid for the others, including me, I need to follow these steps:\n\n1. Identify the Venmo transactions related to the lunch at Enchanted Eats.\n2. Determine the total amount spent by everyone, including my share of $20.\n3. Subtract my share from the total to find out how much my manager paid for the others.\n\nLet me start by exploring the Venmo API to understand how to retrieve transaction data.\n\nCode:\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_descriptions(app_name='venmo'))\n```",

{
  "automatically_repaired_code": "apis.supervisor.complete_task(answer=1074.0)",
  "complete_environment_observation": "<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL_OBSERVATION:SHA256=893e75a4b2a311f8352fcdd7f2742d70db1e1b4e0fea4d0ba8bacd1e1a4eb152>",
  "exact_executed_code": "apis.supervisor.complete_task(answer=1074.0)",
  "exact_model_message_array": [
    {
      "content": "I am your supervisor and you are a super intelligent AI Assistant whose job is to achieve my day-to-day tasks completely autonomously.\n\nTo do this, you will need to interact with app/s (e.g., spotify, venmo etc) using their associated APIs on my behalf. For this you will undertake a *multi-step conversation* using a python REPL environment.       "content": "I am your supervisor and you are a super intelligent AI Assistant whose job is to achieve my day-to-day tasks completely autonomously.\n\nTo do this, you will need to interact with app/s (e.g., spotify, venmo etc) using their associated APIs on my behalf. Let me check the `show_transactions` API to understand how to retrieve all transactions.\n\nCode:\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_doc(app_name='venmo', api_name='show_transactions'))\n```",
      "role": "assistant"
    },

this email address will be shown.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value is email address\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"min_created_at\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Minimum created_at datetime to filter transactions in YYYY-MM-DD format.\",\n   \"default\": \"1500-01-01\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"max_created_at\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Maximum created_at datetime to filter transactions in YYYY-MM-DD format.\",\n   \"default\": \"3000-01-01\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"min_like_count\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Minimum like count to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 0,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"max_like_count\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Maximum like count   "raw_model_response": "I can see that the `show_transactions` API allows me to retrieve all transactions, including those related to the lunch at Enchanted Eats. Since the task mentions that everyone's transactions except mine should be on my social feed, I'll use the `show_social_feed` API to get the transactions of my coworkers and then use the `show_transactions` API to get my own transactions.\n\nLet me start by retrieving the transactions from my social feed.\n\nCode:\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_doc(app_name='venmo', api_name='show_social_feed'))\n```",
